_source line 2957 · 2026-05-22T17:21:26.144757+00:00_

9	\paragraph{Evidence for discriminative compression.}
10	Classification DAPT compresses the feature space into few discriminative dimensions (participation ratio 3.7--9.4 vs.\ 25--32 for MLM/vanilla) with 3--5 orders of magnitude higher separability (Fisher 2.6--19.7 vs.\ $<$0.001; Appendix~\ref{app:representation}). Scrambled labels produce comparable compression (Fisher\,=\,2.6), consistent with \citet{zhang2021understanding}'s framework for how classification modifies representations, with two domain-specific findings: (i)~domain-relevant label partitioning is necessary (topic labels insufficient), and (ii)~compression localizes to 3 SVD dimensions in layers 8--12 (Fisher 15$\times$ vanilla at peak; Appendix~\ref{app:layerwise}), with early layers remaining label-independent as in \citet{maennel2020random}. A discriminative probe confirms: 3 SVD components of 9-class layer-10 representations achieve perfect AA OOD detection (F1\,=\,1.000) vs.\ MLM 0.636 with all 768 dimensions (Appendix~\ref{app:discriminative_probe}). The complexity-dependent boundary persists ($d < 0.1$ on DD/FITD; $d$\,=\,0.44 on AA). Frozen probing near-parity (AA gap 3.1\,pp) confirms discriminative structure is established in Stage-1.
11	
12	\paragraph{Condition-specific advantage.}
13	The evidence hierarchy is specific to multi-turn synthetic evaluation. On ToxicChat (single-turn human data), JB-MLM leads (0.837) while 9-class ranks lower (0.596): 77\% from false positives. Truncating synthetic conversations to a single turn \emph{widens} the 9-class advantage ($\bar{\Delta}$\,=\,+1.6\,pp $\to$ +6.0\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:truncated}), implicating distributional differences between synthetic and human text rather than multi-turn structure. Variance reverses (classification std\,=\,0.087 vs.\ MLM\,=\,0.013); practitioners facing single-turn human data should consider MLM DAPT.
14

3	\section{Discussion}
4	\label{sec:discussion}
5	
6	\paragraph{Confounds and transferability.}
7	Three observations argue against a topic shortcut: unstable topic OOD (F1\,=\,0.541$\pm$0.207), scrambled labels within 3.9\,pp, and label-free MLM detection (F1\,$\geq$\,0.97). ToxicChat (+18--42\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:human_data}) and cross-LLM experiments (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_llm}) confirm transfer; mixed-LLM 9-class maintains DD F1\,=\,0.818$\pm$0.096 while MLM collapses to vanilla level (Appendix~\ref{app:mixed_llm}). Dataset composition---not sample count---is the primary determinant (Appendix~\ref{app:scale_validation}); prompt optimization improves LLM-as-judge OOD detection but does not close the encoder gap (Appendix~\ref{app:prompt_opt}).
8	
9	\paragraph{Evidence for discriminative compression.}
10	Classification DAPT compresses the feature space into few discriminative dimensions (participation ratio 3.7--9.4 vs.\ 25--32 for MLM/vanilla) with 3--5 orders of magnitude higher separability (Fisher 2.6--19.7 vs.\ $<$0.001; Appendix~\ref{app:representation}). Scrambled labels produce comparable compression (Fisher\,=\,2.6), consistent with \citet{zhang2021understanding}'s framework for how classification modifies representations, with two domain-specific findings: (i)~domain-relevant label partitioning is necessary (topic labels insufficient), and (ii)~compression localizes to 3 SVD dimensions in layers 8--12 (Fisher 15$\times$ vanilla at peak; Appendix~\ref{app:layerwise}), with early layers remaining label-independent as in \citet{maennel2020random}. A discriminative probe confirms: 3 SVD components of 9-class layer-10 representations achieve perfect AA OOD detection (F1\,=\,1.000) vs.\ MLM 0.636 with all 768 dimensions (Appendix~\ref{app:discriminative_probe}). The complexity-dependent boundary persists ($d < 0.1$ on DD/FITD; $d$\,=\,0.44 on AA). Frozen probing near-parity (AA gap 3.1\,pp) confir

1	% conclusion.tex — D073 Phase 2 revision (page-compressed)
2	
3	\section{Discussion}
4	\label{sec:discussion}
5	
6	\paragraph{Confounds and transferability.}
7	Three observations argue against a topic shortcut: unstable topic OOD (F1\,=\,0.541$\pm$0.207), scrambled labels within 3.9\,pp, and label-free MLM detection (F1\,$\geq$\,0.97). ToxicChat (+18--42\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:human_data}) and cross-LLM experiments (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_llm}) confirm transfer; mixed-LLM 9-class maintains DD F1\,=\,0.818$\pm$0.096 while MLM collapses to vanilla level (Appendix~\ref{app:mixed_llm}). Dataset composition---not sample count---is the primary determinant (Appendix~\ref{app:scale_validation}); prompt optimization improves LLM-as-judge OOD detection but does not close the encoder gap (Appendix~\ref{app:prompt_opt}).
8	
9	\paragraph{Evidence for discriminative compression.}
10	Classification DAPT compresses features into few discriminative dimensions (participation ratio 3.7--9.4 vs.\ 25--32 for MLM/vanilla; Fisher separability 2.6--19.7 vs.\ $<$0.001; Appendix~\ref{app:representation}). Scrambled labels produce comparable compression (Fisher\,=\,2.6), consistent with \citet{zhang2021understanding}, with two domain-specific findings: (i)~domain-relevant partitioning is necessary (topic labels insufficient), and (ii)~compression localizes to 3 SVD dimensions in layers 8--12 (Appendix~\ref{app:layerwise}). A discriminative probe confirms: 3 SVD components of 9-class layer-10 representations achieve perfect AA OOD detection (F1\,=\,1.000) vs.\ MLM 0.636 with all 768 dimensions (Appendix~\ref{app:discriminative_probe}); frozen probing near-parity (AA gap 3.1\,pp) confirms this structure is established in Stage-1.
11	
12	\paragraph{Condition-specific advantage.}
13	The evidence hierarchy is specific to multi-turn synthetic evaluation. On ToxicChat (single-turn human data), JB-MLM leads (0.837) while 9-class ranks lower (0.596): 77\% from false positives. Truncating synthetic con

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_code_exp61_random_binary_partition_20260522T164029\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"code:exp61_random_binary_partition\", \"project\": \"persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-22T16:40:29.569591+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-22T17:04:45.068178+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"Artifact registered (`art_20260522_81f242`).\\n\\n## EXP-B 结果\\n\\n**EXP-B: Random Binary Partition of 9 Strategy Labels** 已完成，10 个 partition，总耗时 16.1 分钟。\\n\\n| 指标 | Mean ± Std |\\n|------|-----------|\\n| IID F1 | **1.0000 ± 0.0000** |\\n| DD OOD F1 | **1.0000 ± 0.0000** |\\n| AA OOD F1 | **0.9636 ± 0.1093** |\\n\\n9/10 partition 全部三项指标完美，唯一异常是 **seed 7**（A=[1,4,6,7], B=[0,2,3,5,8]）的 AA F1 = 0.6356。\\n\\n**关键结论**：无论如何随机二分策略标签，binary DAPT 的 OOD 泛化能力几乎不受影响。这进一步支持了\\\"DAPT 阶段学到的是领域表征而非标签语义\\\"的论点——即使将 9 类标签坍缩为任意二分，Stage-2 GRU 仍然能在 OOD 数据上泛化。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Q2: 10-seed Scrambled TOST\\n\\n10 个 scrambled permutation seeds: **42, 123, 456, 789, 1000, 2024, 2025, 2026, 314, 628**。\\n\\n完整 10-seed TOST 分析已存在于 `results/exp41_tost_10seed_analysis.json`：\\n- **DD**: 等价（TOST p = 2.06e-9）\\n- **AA**: **不等价**（TOST p = 0.183, Cohen's d = 0.440）\\n- **FITD**: 平凡等价（两组均为 1.0）\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

336	Reducing the DAPT learning rate from $2 \times 10^{-5}$ to $1 \times 10^{-5}$ worsens both DD and AA performance, which rules out over-adaptation as the cause of the regression observed in Table~\ref{tab:cross_encoder}. An MLM-only variant (without classification) produces the same pattern (AA Full = 0.874$\pm$0.014); representation drift from continued pretraining, not classification-specific bias, is the underlying cause. Domain-adaptive fine-tuning narrows the representation space of a sufficiently large encoder and reduces its inherent OOD generalization ability.

110	\paragraph{Domain exposure is the primary mechanism.}
111	MLM bridges $\sim$60\,pp from vanilla (0.312$\pm$0.026 $\to$ 0.914$\pm$0.115) without labels; this supports the DAPT interpretation~\citep{gururangan2020dontstop}. TF-IDF collapse on ActorAttack (0.261 vs.\ 9-class 0.972) confirms that domain-adapted representations capture features beyond lexical overlap.
112	All three classification-supervised variants converge: all tested domain-relevant partitions suffice for near-perfect detection (F1 = 0.994--1.000 on DD). Extended to $n$\,=\,10 seeds (each using an independent random permutation), TOST (margin = 5\,pp) confirms equivalence on DD ($p < 10^{-8}$, $d$\,=\,$-$0.14; Table~\ref{tab:tost_sensitivity}) and FITD ($p < 10^{-8}$, $d \approx 0$), with a complexity-dependent boundary: template attacks show full equivalence ($d < 0.1$), while structurally complex ActorAttack retains moderate label sensitivity ($d$\,=\,0.44, $p$\,=\,0.183; 9-class 0.979 vs.\ scrambled 0.949). Label granularity (binary vs.\ 9-class) is a non-factor across all families.
113	We observed 4--20$\times$ lower cross-seed variance for classification supervision over MLM on synthetic OOD (std 0.005 vs.\ 0.115 on DD; observed across 3 seeds, with consistent directionality across 5 seeds on a second GPU, $n$\,=\,5, 4.6$\times$; formal variance ratio tests are not applicable at these sample sizes; cross-seed ensemble yields marginal additional gains, Appendix~\ref{app:ensemble}). This stability benefit does not transfer to human-authored data, where classification-supervised variants exhibit the highest variance and pure MLM the lowest (Section~\ref{sec:human_data}).
114	Appendix~\ref{app:tsne} provides t-SNE visualizations confirming that domain-adapted variants produce tighter, more separable embedding clusters than the vanilla baseline.

1	% conclusion.tex — D073 Phase 2 revision (page-compressed)
2	
3	\section{Discussion}
4	\label{sec:discussion}
5	
6	\paragraph{Confounds and transferability.}
7	Three observations argue against a topic shortcut: unstable topic OOD (F1\,=\,0.541$\pm$0.207), scrambled labels within 3.9\,pp, and label-free MLM detection (F1\,$\geq$\,0.97). ToxicChat (+18--42\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:human_data}) and cross-LLM experiments (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_llm}) confirm transfer; mixed-LLM 9-class maintains DD F1\,=\,0.818$\pm$0.096 while MLM collapses to vanilla level (Appendix~\ref{app:mixed_llm}). Dataset composition---not sample count---is the primary determinant (Appendix~\ref{app:scale_validation}); prompt optimization does not close the encoder gap (Appendix~\ref{app:prompt_opt}).
8	
9	\paragraph{Evidence for discriminative compression.}
10	Classification DAPT compresses features into few discriminative dimensions (participation ratio 3.7--9.4 vs.\ 25--32 for MLM/vanilla; Fisher separability 2.6--19.7 vs.\ $<$0.001; Appendix~\ref{app:representation}). Scrambled labels produce comparable compression (Fisher\,=\,2.6), consistent with \citet{zhang2021understanding}, with two domain-specific findings: (i)~domain-relevant partitioning is necessary (topic labels insufficient), and (ii)~compression localizes to 3 SVD dimensions in layers 8--12 (Appendix~\ref{app:layerwise}). A discriminative probe over these 3 dimensions achieves perfect AA OOD detection vs.\ MLM 0.636 with all 768 (Appendix~\ref{app:discriminative_probe}); frozen probing near-parity (AA gap 3.1\,pp) confirms this structure is established in Stage-1.
11	
12	\paragraph{Condition-specific advantage.}
13	The evidence hierarchy is specific to multi-turn synthetic evaluation. On ToxicChat (single-turn human data), JB-MLM leads (0.837) while 9-class ranks lower (0.596): 77\% from false positives.
